上成立。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: can a single language model retain the two practical strengths of autoregressive modeling—stable next-token learning and KV-cache-friendly inference—while also gaining some of the parallel generation benefits of masked diffusion models, without inheriting the usual MDM failure modes? Those failure modes are central in the paper: no KV-cache reuse at inference, and weak coherence because denoising must model dependencies over a combinatorially hard space of masked token configurations. The proposed answer is not merely to add two losses together. Zarya reorganizes training examples into variable-size slots and uses a curriculum that gradually increases slot granularity, so the model moves from fine-grained AR-style learning toward coarse-grained diffusion-style reconstruction in a controlled way.","importance":"$20","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"$23","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应读方法和推理部分；slot curriculum 若真能稳定连接 AR 与 MDM，会改变 diffusion LM 只能另起炉灶的预设。","en":"Read the method and inference sections; if the slot curriculum really bridges AR and MDM cleanly, it weakens the prior that diffusion LMs need a separate modeling stack."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Cohere"],"authors":["Leonid Sinev","Ilya Koziev","Vladislav Leshchuk"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-LM","autoregressive","masked-diffusion","speculative-decoding","hybrid-model"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19868","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.19868","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19717","title":"Learn Your Own Thoughts: Abstract Token Curriculum","abstract":"Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable reasoning capabilities by utilizing chain-of-thought (CoT) as a scratchpad for intermediate stages of thinking. 数据，也不必把所有中间计算都暴露成自然语言。这样做有几层意义。第一，它给 latent reasoning 提供了一个更可训练的 recipe：不是直接要求模型在 hardest distribution 上自发形成抽象思维，而是通过 curriculum 逐步搭脚手架。第二，它把理论和工程连起来，回应了 attention simplicity bias 这类关于“哪些 CoT 容易被学到”的问题。第三，它提出的 backtracking 和 truncated BPTT 处理了 latent chain 训练里很实际的两个瓶颈：旧阶段表示被新阶段破坏，以及链长增长带来的显存/算力成本。贡献量级上，这更像一个新训练范式雏形，而不是单点技巧；对未来无显式 CoT 的 reasoning pretrain、mid-train 课程设计、latent token 的实证版本。\n\n在图任务和多位数算术上，ATC 相比无课程、固定难度训练、以及若干已有基线，表现出更好的样本效率和更高的最终任务成功率。论文还做了关键消融：去掉 curriculum backtracking 后，随着课程推进，旧阶段能力会明显回退，说明早期 latent 表示确实会被后续训练破坏；去掉 truncated BPTT 虽然理论上梯度更完整，但训练成本迅速上升，链长扩展受限。加入 chain-of-thought supervision, hand-designed scratchpads, or manually specified latent semantics. Standard CoT works well, but it depends on natural-language traces or task-specific intermediate formats. The authors argue that two obstacles remain. First, many hard reasoning tasks are difficult to learn end-to-end from final answers alone. Second, even if latent thought tokens are inserted, the model may not automatically use them as stable computational states. The core problem is therefore how to train continuous abstract thought tokens so that they become functional intermediate representations rather than empty placeholders.","importance":"For pretraining and reasoning research, the significance is that it tries to decouple intermediate computation from explicit textual supervision. Finally, the paper addresses two practical bottlenecks in latent-chain training: preserving earlier learned representations as the curriculum advances, and controlling the memory/compute cost of long reasoning chains. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T15:36:53Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20581.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论离散 diffusion LM 中一个容易被忽略的采样偏差：模型每一步用逐位置分布同时写入多个 token，但真实数据中的这些位置往往在条件于已固定 token 后仍然相关。只要被同时写入的位置不是条件独立的，哪怕模型的每个单点条件分布都已经学到精确值，采样步骤仍然无法匹配训练分布。","importance":"这对 diffusion LM、masked LM、remasking sampler 和 any-order 生成都有直接影响。论文的贡献不在提出更好的 sampler，而在给出一个负面边界：逐位置置信度不足以判断哪些 token 可以并行写入。对预训练研究来说，这解释了为什么一些非 AR 生成模型在格式正确率、任务正确率都很高时，仍可能生成错误的联合分布。它也提示 diffusion LM 的关键瓶颈可能不只是 denoiser 质量，而是采样器使用的统计量太弱：单点 marginal 无法表达 token group 的依赖结构。","technical_approach":"论文把常见离散 diffusion 采样步骤统一成 independent-update sampler：网络在每个可写位置输出一个分布，调度器根据这些逐位置分布选择一组位置 R，然后对 R 中各位置独立采样。这个定义覆盖 absorbing-state masked diffusion、remasking diffusion，以及 uniform-state diffusion 的终端阶段。核心理论结果有四个。第一，如果 R 中位置在给定冻结 token 后存在条件依赖，则一步生成分布与真实条件分布的差距至少等于该 group 的 total correlation。第二，没有任何逐位置分布集合能够判断一个 group 是否独立，因为两个 joint distribution 可以有相同的 per-position marginals，但合法组合完全不同。第三，dependent step 产生的状态处在训练目标没有约束好的区域，per-position masked cross-entropy 并不会为这些状态指定正确 joint。第四，在硬约束数据上，如果 sampler 每次只改一个位置，一些由其余位置唯一钉住的 token 也可能不可达，说明简单退回完全串行也不是完整解法。论文的重点是把“置信度高就可以并行写”这个经验规则拆开：高置信度只说明单点分布尖锐，不说明多个位置之间可以独立采样。","experiments":"实验使用 ScanAndAdd，一个 joint distribution、conditional entropy、support 都可计算的合成任务。这样可以区分“样本看起来正确”和“生成密度是否正确”。模型训练后很早就达到 1.00 的 well-formedness、correctness 和 uniqueness，但这不代表采样分布正确。使用模型自身置信度选择每步写入位置时，生成 token 分布在 TVT 指标上仍远离训练分布；文中给出的模型达到 1.0 correctness，但 TVT 是 sampling-noise floor 的 29 倍。相同模型如果改用人工指定的写入顺序，TVT 已经无法和训练数据区分，同时 correctness 仍保持在 0.987 到 0.996。这个对比很关键：问题不是 denoiser 没学会，也不是任务太难，而是 confidence-based parallel writing 会把相关位置拆成独立采样，导致密度系统性偏移。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Section 2 的 sampler 形式化定义和 Theorem 3、Theorem 4。Theorem 3 给出 dependent group 的下界，是全文最核心的数学点；Theorem 4 解释为什么任何只看 per-position distribution 的调度器都无法可靠识别独立 group。Section 5 和 Figure 1 要重点看，它们说明 correctness、well-formedness、uniqueness 这类单样本指标为什么会掩盖分布失真。Appendix E 如果有时间也应读，它关系到 per-step 偏差如何在完整采样轨迹中累积。读这篇时不必把重点放在 ScanAndAdd 本身，而要关注它如何构造出“单点条件正确但联合分布错误”的可观测案例。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a structural failure mode in discrete diffusion language models: a sampler often writes multiple token positions in one step using independent draws from per-position distributions, while the true data distribution may still couple those positions after conditioning on the already fixed tokens. If the written positions are conditionally dependent, the step cannot match the training distribution, even when every per-position conditional is exact.","importance":"The result is directly relevant to diffusion LMs, masked generation, remasking samplers, and any-order generation. Its main contribution is a boundary result rather than a new model: per-position confidence is not enough to decide which tokens can be safely written in parallel. For pretraining research, this helps explain why non-autoregressive or semi-parallel generative models can achieve high validity and task accuracy while still producing the wrong density. The limitation is not only the denoiser; it is also the information exposed to the scheduler. Marginals do not encode the dependency structure of token groups.","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"The empirical study uses ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task with a closed-form joint distribution, computable conditionals, entropies, and support. This choice lets the authors separate sample-level correctness from density correctness. A trained model reaches 1.00 well-formedness, correctness, and uniqueness early in training. Yet when the sampler chooses positions using its own confidence scores, the generated distribution remains far from the training distribution: in the reported run, correctness is 1.0, but TVT is 29 times above the sampling-noise floor. With the same trained model and a hand-specified write order, TVT becomes indistinguishable from the training data while correctness remains between 0.987 and 0.996. This contrast isolates the failure to the parallel confidence-driven scheduler rather than to model capacity or denoising accuracy.","reading_guide":"Read Section 2 carefully, especially the unified sampler definition and Theorems 3 and 4. Theorem 3 is the central lower bound for dependent writes. Theorem 4 explains why schedulers that only inspect per-position distributions cannot reliably identify independent groups. Figure 1 and Section 5 are the key empirical pieces, because they show why correctness, well-formedness, and uniqueness can hide a density error. Appendix E is useful for understanding how per-step costs accumulate over a full trajectory. The useful lens is not the synthetic task itself, but the constructed separation between exact marginals and incorrect joint generation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会直接动到你对 discrete diffusion 并行写 token 的 prior：若训练和采样都默认逐位置独立，很多“更快生成”可能先天带偏；建议先复现它的反例，再决定是否继续押注 remasking 路线。","en":"This one should update your prior on discrete diffusion parallel writes: if training and sampling assume per-position independence, many 'faster generation' schemes may be biased by construction; reproduce the counterexample before betting further on remasking."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Russ Webb","Amitis Shidani","Alice Bizeul","Dan Busbridge"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion-lm","discrete-diffusion","parallel-decoding","sampling-bias","theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20581","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.20751","title":"dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models","abstract":"Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). This is nontrivial because AR-to-DLM adaptation usually makes attention bidirectional, while RNN layers are structurally causal and cannot be bidirectionalized by changing a mask.","importance":"The main value is that it connects two trends that have mostly been studied separately: cheaper AR-to-DLM adaptation and the move from full-attention AR models to hybrid attention-recurrent architectures. The result is useful for pretraining research because it suggests that a causal recurrent bias is not necessarily incompatible with any-order generation. It also gives a practical recipe for obtaining DLMs from existing AR checkpoints with tens of billions of adaptation tokens, rather than training DLMs from scratch with trillion-token budgets.","technical_approach":"The adaptation is deliberately minimal. For Qwen3.5 models at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, the attention layers are made bidirectional, while the RNN layers are left causal. The models are then continued with a diffusion language modeling objective under a shared recipe. The paper argues that this design is reasonable because language and even trained DLMs show a strong left-to-right tendency at the global level, although they can decode nearby positions out of order. A full-attention Qwen3-1.7B control with a similar trunk size is adapted under the same recipe to separate the effect of hybrid architecture from the effect of scale.","experiments":"The paper reports several informative measurements. On decoding order, all dQwen3.5 scales show DLM-like behavior: local AR-ness is around 0.64, within the 0.58–0.66 range reported for other DLMs, while global AR-ness is much higher, roughly 0.88–0.97. On adaptation efficiency, dQwen3.5-2B reaches the same training loss as the trunk-matched full-attention control with about half the tokens. On downstream benchmarks, dQwen3.5-2B after 50B adaptation tokens outperforms CoDA, trained with 200B tokens, on 6 of 7 benchmarks. At the larger scale, dQwen3.5-9B after 50B tokens achieves the best result on 4 of 7 benchmarks when compared with Dream-7B, Dream-Coder-7B, and LLaDA-8B, whose token budgets are 580B, 322B, and 2.3T respectively. Longer adaptation is not uniformly beneficial: increasing from 50B to 100B improves 5 of 7 benchmarks at 0.8B, but only 1 of 7 at 9B.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30 to 45 minutes. Start with Section 3 for the adaptation recipe: which layers are changed, which layers are kept causal, and how the diffusion objective is applied. Then read Section 4 for the main evidence, especially Figure 1 on decoding and parallel speedups, Table 3 on speedup versus accuracy, and Figure 8 on local and global AR-ness. Section 5 is worth reading for the architectural interpretation. The two questions worth deeper follow-up are why the hybrid backbone adapts faster than the full-attention control, and why 100B-token adaptation helps small models more consistently than the 9B model.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"证明了混合架构（RNN+Attention）也能做 Diffusion LM 且收敛更快，打破了非因果建模必须用全注意力的 prior，值得关注其双向化处理细节。","en":"Proves hybrid architectures (RNN+Attention) can be efficient DLMs with faster convergence, breaking the prior that non-causal modeling requires full attention; worth checking the bidirectionalization details."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Texas at Austin","Affiliation:University of Texas at Austin"],"authors":["Anton Xue","Litu Rout","Aditya Akella","Adam Klivans","Sujay Sanghavi","Sanjay Shakkottai"],"notable_authors":["Sanjay Shakkottai","Adam Klivans","Sujay Sanghavi"],"tags":["Diffusion Language Model","Hybrid Architecture","Qwen3.5","Non-causal Modeling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20751","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19356","title":"How to Guide Your Language Flow","abstract":"We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where p","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T19:29:07Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19356.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决连续 diffusion / flow language model 中 guidance 难以高效、稳定迁移的问题：已有 CFG/self-conditioning 需要额外前向或额外训练分支，autoguidance 又依赖一个动态匹配的弱模型，在语言扩散模型里不容易构造。","importance":"对新型语言建模范式有直接参考价值。它不是提出新的 dLM 训练目标，而是把 diffusion 模型里很关键的推理时控制手段做成了更适合语言模型扩展的版本。贡献主要有三点：第一，用冻结中间特征训练 probe，降低 autoguidance 的双模型推理成本；第二，在连续 dLM 的无条件生成和 likelihood-style QA 评测上验证 guidance 可以带来稳定收益；第三，通过 probe 分析弱模型来源，指出有效 autoguidance 需要弱模型来自训练早期的低熵阶段，而不是任意 dropout 或简单降配模型。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是把强模型 trunk 的隐藏状态当作固定表示，在某一层或多层接一个轻量 MLP probe，训练它预测弱模型输出。主模型参数冻结或 stop-gradient，不让 probe 的梯度回流到 trunk。这样 probe 相当于共享强模型动力学的弱分支，而不是另起一个弱模型。训练目标随底座 dLM 而变：对 FLM 这类 data-space 模型可用 cross-entropy divergence，对 ELF 这类 latent-space 模型可用 MSE。推理时同时得到强输出 x_s 和 probe 弱输出 x_w，用 x_s - x_w 作为 guidance 方向；λ=1 是原始强模型，λ>1 沿强弱差值外推。与 CFG/self-conditioning 相比，它不需要额外 unconditional pass；与标准 autoguidance 相比，它避免完整弱模型前向，只增加一个 attention-free MLP head。这个设计的关键取舍是牺牲一部分弱模型独立性，换取与强模型更一致的内部动态和更低推理成本。","experiments":"实验覆盖 latent-space 和 data-space 连续语言扩散模型。论文报告 probe 的额外推理开销约为模型计算量的 2%，训练 probe 只需约 1% 额外训练计算；相对标准 autoguidance，推理 FLOPs 减少约 1.4–2.0 倍。在 ELF 上，probe guidance 的无条件生成达到 GenPPL 23、entropy 5.40 的质量-多样性点，并优于 self-conditioning 的 Pareto 曲线。在 FLM 上，probe guidance 也优于原有 autoguided Pareto frontier。对 1.7B 连续 dLM，作者把 likelihood estimator 改造成可接受 guidance 的形式，并在多个 multiple-choice QA benchmark 上得到一致提升。一个有信息量的分析结果是：dLM 训练中存在快速 entropy increase 阶段；有效 autoguidance 的弱模型需要来自 climbout 之前的低熵区域，否则强弱差值不能形成可靠 guidance 方向。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 中对 CFG、self-conditioning、autoguidance 在 dLM 上的问题定义，能快速理解为什么 probe 不是普通 early-exit head。然后看 Method 里 stop-gradient probe 训练和 guided velocity 的公式，重点关注 λ 的定义以及 x-space prediction 到 velocity update 的转换。实验部分建议重点看 ELF/FLM 的 quality-diversity Pareto 图、1.7B multiple-choice likelihood 评测，以及 guidance dynamics 分析；后者对理解“弱模型必须弱在哪里”最有价值。如果只关心预训练范式，优先读训练动态和 probe layer/weakness 控制相关消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical bottleneck in continuous diffusion and flow language models: guidance is useful, but existing options either require an extra forward pass, introduce awkward self-conditioning machinery, or rely on a weak model whose dynamics do not match the primary language diffusion model.","importance":"The work is relevant to foundation-model pretraining research because guidance is one of the main levers that makes diffusion-style generative modeling usable at inference time. The contribution is not a new language-modeling objective; it is a cleaner interface between a pretrained continuous dLM and inference-time control. It also gives a useful diagnostic result: for autoguidance in language, the weak model should come from an early low-entropy regime rather than from an arbitrary degraded copy.","technical_approach":"Probe guidance trains lightweight MLP probes on frozen hidden states of an existing dLM. Gradients are stopped at the trunk, so the probe learns a weak prediction head without changing the primary model. The probe objective follows the underlying flow model loss: for example, cross-entropy-style divergence for FLM-like data-space models and MSE for ELF-like latent-space models. At inference, the model computes the strong output x_s and the probe output x_w from the same forward computation. The generation update extrapolates along x_s - x_w, with λ=1 recovering the unguided model and λ>1 increasing guidance strength. The key difference from standard autoguidance is that the weak path is not a separate full model; it shares the strong model’s representations and adds only an attention-free MLP computation.","experiments":"The reported overhead is small: probe inference costs roughly 2% of model compute, and probe training adds about 1% extra training compute. Compared with standard autoguidance, the method uses about 1.4–2.0 times fewer FLOPs at inference. On ELF, probe guidance reaches a GenPPL of 23 at entropy 5.40 and improves the quality-diversity trade-off over self-conditioning. On FLM, it improves over the autoguided Pareto frontier. On a 1.7B continuous dLM, the authors adapt likelihood estimation to support guidance and report consistent gains across multiple-choice QA benchmarks. The training-dynamics study is especially useful: both latent-space and data-space dLMs show a rapid entropy increase during training, and successful autoguidance depends on choosing a weak model from the low-entropy phase before that climb.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough for the main idea. Read the introduction for the comparison with CFG, self-conditioning, and autoguidance. Then focus on the method equations for probe training, stop-gradient usage, and guided velocity construction. In the experiments, prioritize the ELF and FLM quality-diversity curves, the 1.7B multiple-choice evaluation, and the guidance-dynamics section. Yet this design is largely a convention rather than a tested conclusion. When multiple sequence mixers are combined in one stack, improvements may arise from mechanism choice, placement, or both, making causal attribution difficult. We introduce Aether-7B-5Attn, a 6.59B-parameter mixture-of-experts model ($\\approx$2.98B active) whose 49 layers contain seven sequence-mixing mechanisms arranged as a $7\\times7$ ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T06:39:03Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20269.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“再发明一种 sequence mixer”，而是一个更基础、也更容易被忽略的问题：当一个深层语言模型同时混用多种 mixer 时，性能提升到底来自机制种类本身，还是来自它们在深度上的摆放位置。现有 hybrid 架构通常把两件事绑在一起改：一边换 mixer，一边改 layer schedule，结果很难做因果归因。作者把这个问题形式化为 composition、placement、parameter allocation 三个因素的解耦，并提出用 Latin square 作为一种可证明平衡的层级排布方式，让每种 mixer 在每个深度区段都获得等量暴露，从设计上消掉 placement confound。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它给 heterogeneous stack 提供了一个比“手工排层”更干净的实验工具。很多混合架构论文声称某种层间搭配有效，但没有办法说明是 mixer 本身有效，还是恰好放在了合适深度。Latin square 至少提供了一个 search-free、可复现实验基线。第二，它给架构搜索的目标函数提了个醒：在作者的设定里，性能主要由“分布式的异质组合”决定，而不是某个精细排序。这意味着未来做 hybrid pretrain 时，也许不必把大量算力花在 layer-by-layer 排列搜索上，先保证 balanced composition 可能更重要。贡献量级上，它更像一个架构实验方法学和设计原则，而不是已经证明可大规模替代主流 Transformer 的定型方案。","technical_approach":"方法核心很直接：把 N 种 sequence mixer 排成一个 N×N 的 Latin square，对应 N^2 层网络。Latin square 的约束是每一行、每一列每种符号都恰好出现一次。映射到网络深度后，可以理解为：无论把 49 层切成 7 个连续 depth band，还是按固定步长抽样，每种 mixer 的出现频次都严格均衡，因此不会出现“某个 mixer 主要集中在浅层/深层”的偏置。\n\n旗舰模型 Aether-7B-5Attn 是一个 49 层、6.59B 参数的 MoE 语言模型，活跃参数约 2.98B。文中强调它包含 7 种 sequence-mixing mechanism，并按 7×7 Latin square 排布。摘要里写 5Attn，但正文明确说是 seven mechanisms；从命名看，较可能是 5 类 attention 变体再加上其他 mixer，但给出的全文片段没有完整列出具体 7 种机制名称，因此这里不能替作者补全。更重要的是，论文并不把新 mixer 作为卖点，而是把“平衡排布”作为主变量。\n\n为了验证原则而不是只展示一个大模型，作者又构造了一个参数匹配的 proxy：4 种 mixer、4×4 Latin square、16 层。围绕这个 proxy 做多 seed 对照，系统比较三类变体：\n1. permutation variant：保持 balanced distributed schedule，只改变同一组 mixer 的具体顺序；\n2. banded / contiguous variant：把同类 mixer 聚成连续深度区段，破坏分布均衡但不改变组成；\n3. homogeneous variant：用单一 mixer 替代 heterogeneous composition。\n\n这个设计的关键点在于，它把“顺序是否重要”和“是否需要全深度分散混合”拆开了。相比常见 hybrid 模型直接给一个经验式 schedule，这种实验设计更接近受控变量实验。作者还补了一项工程上有用的分析：给出 heterogeneous stack 中各 mixer 在不同 context length 下的 cost profile，用来说明混合架构的代价并不只体现在参数量，还体现在长度相关的吞吐和显存曲线。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容看，最关键的实验结论有三个，而且方向很清楚。\n\n第一，balanced distributed schedule 内部的 permutation 几乎不影响验证损失。作者明确说“reordering mechanisms within a balanced distributed schedule produces negligible changes in validation loss”。这说明只要每种 mixer 在深度上分布均衡，具体谁在第几层的细粒度顺序不是主要矛盾。\n\n第二，把 mixer 集中成 contiguous depth bands 会明显变差。也就是同样的机制集合，如果按“前几层一种、后几层另一种”的方式堆叠，效果不如在全深度范围内交错分布。这一点对很多手工 hybrid 设计很关键，因为很多现有做法默认浅层局部、深层全局，或者前半段 SSM、后半段 attention，但这篇工作提示这种集中式分区未必是好默认值。\n\n第三，heterogeneous stack 改成 homogeneous stack 的退化最大。作者直接说 homogeneous replacement incurs the largest penalty，说明性能收益主要来自 composition 本身，而不是 placement 微调。\n\n规模上，旗舰模型是 6.59B 参数、约 2.98B active 的 49 层 MoE；受控消融则使用参数匹配 proxy 和 multi-seed 训练。由于提供的全文片段没有包含具体 loss 数值、下游 benchmark 分数、训练 token 数、各 mixer FLOPs/latency 表格，因此不能编造具体提升百分比。可以确认的是，论文把 validation loss 作为核心判据，并且同时做了 cost profiling，而不是只报任务分数。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议抓三部分。\n\n第一看 Introduction 和方法定义，重点不是背景综述，而是作者如何把 heterogeneous architecture 的收益拆成 composition、placement、parameter allocation 三个因素。这个问题定义得是否干净，决定了整篇工作的价值。\n\n第二看 proxy ablation 的实验设计和对应图表。要重点确认三组对照是否真正参数匹配、训练预算一致、随机种子充分，以及“balanced permutation 无差异、banded 变差、homogeneous 最差”这三个结论是否在多个 seed 下稳定。这里是整篇文章最有信息量的部分，预计 20–30 分钟。\n\n第三看 per-mechanism cost profile。即便不打算采用 Latin square，本节也值得扫一遍，因为 heterogeneous stack 的实际代价往往被平均 FLOPs 掩盖。要看作者是否区分了不同 context length 下的吞吐、显存、激活开销，以及这些成本是否会反过来影响最优 mixer 配比。\n\n如果时间有限，可以先跳过旗舰 Aether-7B-5Attn 的展示部分，先读 proxy 实验；因为这篇工作的核心不是“7B 模型做到了什么分数”，而是“怎样做无混淆的异质架构实验”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a methodological problem rather than proposing yet another sequence mixer. Once multiple mixers are combined in a deep LM, any gain can come from three entangled sources: which mixers are included, where they are placed across depth, and how parameters are allocated. Most prior hybrid architectures change all three at once, so causal attribution is weak. The paper turns this into a controlled design problem and uses a Latin-square construction to guarantee balanced depth-wise exposure for each mixer, removing placement asymmetry by design.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mainly methodological. First, it provides a cleaner baseline for studying heterogeneous stacks than hand-crafted layer schedules. If a hybrid model improves, this setup helps answer whether the gain comes from composition itself or from a lucky placement pattern. Second, it suggests that architecture search for hybrid stacks may be over-focusing on exact layer ordering. In the reported setting, performance is driven much more by balanced distributed composition than by fine-grained permutation. That does not make Latin square the final answer for large-scale LM design, but it is a useful experimental instrument and a practical design prior.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"$2a","reading_guide":"$2b","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得读方法和消融设计：Latin square 不是炫技，它能减少混合架构里最常见的 placement confound。","en":"Read the method and ablations: the Latin-square setup is not cosmetic; it attacks a real placement confound in hybrid mixer studies."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Taebong Kim","Youngsik Hong","Minsik Kim","Sunyoung Choi","Jaewon Jang","Minseo Kim"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["hybrid-architecture","sequence-mixers","moe","latin-square","ablation-design"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20269","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20269","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.20530","title":"Relational Attention for Data-Efficient Language Modeling","abstract":"We present Relational BabyLM, a system submission to the BabyLM 2026 challenge that combines two cognitively motivated inductive biases in a single decoder-only Transformer. Relational a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T15:05:19Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20530.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究低数据语言建模中，标准 self-attention 是否因为把词汇特征和关系结构耦合在同一个注意力通道里，导致结构泛化和样本效率不足。它进一步检验 Dual Attention Transformer 和 Next-Latent Prediction 这两个归纳偏置，在 BabyLM 这种 10M/100M word 训练预算下是否能带来稳定收益。","importance":"这篇工作不是面向前沿大模型规模的训练 recipe，但对架构归纳偏置和数据效率有启发。它把关系结构显式放进 attention，而不是期待模型从少量数据中自己学出关系变量；同时用辅助 hidden-state dynamics 目标推动表示更像对历史的压缩状态。对预训练研究的价值在于：它提供了一个小规模、受控的证据，说明结构化注意力主要改善语法和关系泛化，而辅助 latent objective 更影响人类阅读指标和下游微调表现。","technical_approach":"核心架构是作者重新实现的 Dual Attention Transformer。普通 self-attention 通过 query-key 相似度选择 source token，并取回由同一 source token 生成的 value，因此对象特征和关系信息混在一起。DAT 保留 sensory self-attention 来路由词汇或对象级特征，同时加入 relational attention：source selection 仍类似注意力，但取回的是接收 token 与 source token 之间的显式 pairwise relation vector，以及表示 source 的抽象 symbol。训练上加入 NextLat 辅助目标：一个小的 dynamics model 根据当前 hidden state 和真实 next token 预测下一步 hidden state，训练后丢弃，因此推理仍是标准自回归。作者还比较了多种 symbol retrieval 机制，包括无额外参数的 RoPE-based relative symbols。","experiments":"实验包含 BabyLM strict 100M-word 和 strict-small 10M-word 两个赛道，并在 strict-small 上做了受控矩阵实验。架构因素对结构泛化影响最大：DAT 相比匹配的标准 Transformer 在 BLiMP 上更好，增益集中在 island effects、subject-verb agreement 等结构性子域；但增加 relational stream 的 head 比例并没有继续带来收益，说明有关系通道比盲目加大关系通道更关键。NextLat 的收益更偏认知和下游任务：它解释了更多人类阅读测量方差，并提升 7 个 fine-tuned SuperGLUE 任务中的 5 个。symbol 机制的五种子实验显示，relational-symbolic 变体表现较差，其余机制差异不明显；RoPE-based relative symbols 在无额外参数下接近 learned relative symbol library。100M-word 赛道中，两个最强模型进入当时官方榜单前十，最好模型 overall 排第 6/55、NLP-task subset 排第 3/55；10M-word 赛道中，NextLat submission 在 BLiMP 排第 8/127，在 EWoK 排第 6。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25 到 35 self-attention is a poor inductive bias for low-data language modeling because it entangles lexical object features with relational structure. It tests whether Dual Attention Transformer layers and a next-latent auxiliary objective improve data efficiency and structural generalization under the BabyLM 10M- and 100M-word settings.","importance":"This is not a frontier-scale pretraining recipe, but it is useful for thinking about architectural bias under scarce data. The main signal is that explicit relational attention helps syntactic and structural generalization, while the auxiliary latent-dynamics objective is more connected to human reading measures and downstream finetuning. For pretraining researchers, the paper is a compact experiment on whether relation-aware routing can reduce the amount of data needed to learn compositional structure.","technical_approach":"The model reimplements the Dual Attention Transformer. In ordinary self-attention, the same token representation determines source selection and provides the routed value, so object-level content and relation-level information are coupled. DAT keeps sensory attention for lexical or object features and adds relational attention, where the selected values are explicit pairwise relation vectors plus an abstract source symbol. The training intervention is Next-Latent Prediction: a small dynamics model predicts the next hidden state from the current hidden state and the actual next token. This auxiliary model is discarded after training, so autoregressive inference is unchanged. The paper also compares several symbol-retrieval mechanisms, including a RoPE-based relative-symbol design with no extra parameters.","experiments":"The experiments cover BabyLM strict with 100M words and strict-small with 10M words, with a controlled matrix on strict-small. DAT is the main driver of structural generalization, improving BLiMP relative to matched standard Transformers, especially on island effects and subject–verb agreement. Increasing the fraction of relational heads does not add further gains, suggesting that the presence of a relational stream matters more than its size. NextLat explains more variance in human reading measures and improves 5 of 7 finetuned SuperGLUE tasks. In a five-seed comparison of seven symbol mechanisms, relational-symbolic variants underperform, while the remaining mechanisms are hard to distinguish; the RoPE-based relative symbols match a learned relative-symbol library without adding parameters. On the 100M-word track, the best model ranks 6th of 55 overall and 3rd of 55 on the NLP-task subset at the time of writing; on strict-small, the NextLat submission ranks 8th of 127 on BLiMP and 6th on EWoK.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T20:05:11Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19384.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.MM"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很受限的问题：如何把已经独立训练好的 ViT 和视觉 SSM 检查点做异构融合。难点不在于普通的权重平均，而在于两类模型的 token mixing 机制、参数语义和张量形状都不一致，直接做 weight-space arithmetic 没有定义。作者试图回答的不是“能不能把两套权重硬凑在一起”，而是“能否先按模块语义对齐，再映射到共享潜空间，在这个空间里做有控制的几何融合”。","importance":"对 LLM 预训练研究的直接价值有限，因为对象是视觉模型，且核心贡献不是训练范式、数据或优化，而是异构 checkpoint fusion。启发主要有两点。第一，作者把“异构模型融合”明确拆成语义分组、潜空间对齐、几何融合、双分支保留四步，这种问题分解方式对未来做 Transformer–SSM、AR–diffusion、甚至多模态 backbone 融合时有参考意义。第二，文中反复强调最终产物不是 training-free merge，而是带门控、需微调的 hybrid，这一点很重要：很多 merge 论文把‘初始化方式’和‘最终模型能力’混在一起，这篇在表述上相对克制。贡献量级偏方法性探索，不属于会直接改变预训练主线的工作。","technical_approach":"方法名是 RLPF，核心流程分四步。1）按语义角色分组参数，而不是逐层逐张量硬对齐。分组包括 patch embedding、sequence mixing、feed-forward、normalization 等。这样做的原因是 ViT 的 attention 投影和 SSM 的状态转移矩阵在形状和算子上都不兼容，只能先退到更粗粒度的功能对应。2）把每组参数投影到共享低秩潜空间。文中把几何选择当成设计超参，而不是宣称原始权重天然服从某种流形结构。3）在潜空间做分组融合：部分组用欧氏平均，部分组用 Lorentz 模型下的双曲几何 barycenter，也就是最小化测地距离平方的 Fréchet mean。作者还引入 geodesic anchor objective，约束融合点与各分支表示的几何关系，避免双曲空间中的不稳定漂移。4）把融合后的潜表示再解码回 ViT 分支和 SSM 分支，推理时保留双分支，并用一个小型 learned gate 融合 logits。这里最关键的区别是：它不是把两模型压成一个统一参数化模型，而是用几何初始化 + 轻量训练得到一个两路混合体。因此它更接近“结构化 hybrid assembly”，而不是经典意义上的 checkpoint merge。与 model soups、task arithmetic、TIES 这类同构方法相比，RLPF 的新意不在融合公式本身，而在于先定义跨架构的 role-based correspondence，再讨论几何平均是否有意义。","experiments":"实验覆盖 CIFAR-10、Oxford-IIIT Pet 和 ImageNet-1K。全文给出的重点不是大规模性能刷新，而是把几个比较容易混淆的比较对象分开：ImageNet 上先报告几何融合初始化后的 pre-fine-tuning 结果，再报告门控 hybrid 微调后的最终结果；同时把欧氏融合、Lorentz 融合、不同参数组策略和门控训练的作用拆开看。能确认的结论是：1）单纯几何初始化本身带来一定可用性，但最终性能主要依赖后续 fine-tuning 和双分支保留；2）不同参数组适合的几何不一样，作者并不声称双曲空间对所有模块都更优；3）门控头对稳定组合两分支输出是必要的。由于给到的全文片段没有完整表格和具体数值，无法可靠复述参数量、latent role-based grouping、latent projection、Lorentz barycenter 和 geodesic anchor 的定义，弄清楚哪些模块用欧氏空间，哪些模块用双曲空间，以及为什么不直接做统一几何。第三是实验部分关于 pre-fine-tuning 与 post-fine-tuning 的拆分，以及与同构 merge baseline 的公平性。预计 20–30 Because every data type maps to the same $(x_d, s, e)$ representation without flattening, a single standard tr","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T11:30:33Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20278.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"如何将文本、知识图谱（KG）和超图等具有不同内部结构的数据，统一表示并输入到标准的 Transformer 中进行联合预训练，而无需针对不同模态设计特定的编码器或展平（flattening）策略？","importance":"提出了一种新型的统一数据表示范式 LIS（Labeled Incidence Structures），挑战了传统的将结构化数据展平为序列的 AR 建模方式。这为跨模态（文本+图谱）的统一预训练提供了一个优雅的代数基础，使得标准 Transformer 可以原生处理复杂图结构数据，属于典型的“新型语言建模范式”研究。","technical_approach":"核心创新是 LIS 表示法。它将数据中的每个元素（如 KG 中的实体、文本中的 token）表示为一个三元组 $(x_d, s, e)$：内容 $x_d$、角色/槽位 $s$（如 head, tail, position）和关系实例 $e$（如具体的 triple 或 sentence）。通过组合槽位算子 $R_s$ 和实例算子 $R_e$，为每个元素分配一个结构化地址 $A(s,e) = R_s R_e$。在 Transformer 的注意力机制中，通过“旅程算子”（journey operator）$P_{j\\to i} = A_i^{-1} A_j$ 来计算任意两个 token 之间的结构关系。这种设计将结构差异完全交由算子处理，而模型架构保持不变。","experiments":"理论上证明了简单的加性编码（如将位置和关系分开编码后相加）会丢失联合信息（Theorem 3.2），且基于位置的评分机制在处理大规模知识库时存在顺序依赖和泛化问题（Theorem 4.3, 4.5）。通过合成数据和真实数据的单种子诊断实验（附录 H），初步验证了在文本和 KG 上联合训练单一 Transformer 的可行性。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Section 2 中 LIS 的定义和代数基础，理解结构化地址 $A(s,e)$ 和旅程算子 $P_{j\\to i}$ 的设计。Section 3 关于加性编码缺陷的理论证明解释了“为什么必须这么做”。这篇论文偏理论和架构设计，实验部分较弱，但其统一建模的思想对预训练研究很有启发。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to uniformly represent and input data with different internal structures, such as text, knowledge graphs (KGs), and hypergraphs, into a standard Transformer for joint pre-training, without designing specific encoders or flattening strategies for different modalities?","importance":"Proposes a novel unified data representation paradigm, LIS (Labeled Incidence Structures), challenging the traditional AR modeling approach of flattening structured data into sequences. This provides an elegant algebraic foundation for cross-modal (text + graph) unified pre-training, enabling standard Transformers to natively process complex graph-structured data, falling squarely into the 'novel language modeling paradigms' research area.","technical_approach":"The core innovation is the LIS representation. It represents each element in the data (e.g., an entity in a KG, a token in text) as a triplet $(x_d, s, e)$: content $x_d$, role/slot $s$ (e.g., head, tail, position), and relation instance $e$ (e.g., a specific triple or sentence). By composing a slot operator $R_s$ and an instance operator $R_e$, it assigns a structural address $A(s,e) = R_s R_e$ to each element. In the Transformer's attention mechanism, the structural relationship between any two tokens is computed via a 'journey operator' $P_{j\\to i} = A_i^{-1} A_j$. This design delegates structural differences entirely to the operators, keeping the model architecture unchanged.","experiments":"Theoretically proves that simple additive encodings (e.g., encoding position and relation separately and adding them) lose joint information (Theorem 3.2), and that position-based scoring mechanisms suffer from order dependence and generalization issues when handling large-scale knowledge bases (Theorems 4.3, 4.5). Single-seed diagnostic experiments on synthetic and real data (Appendix H) preliminarily validate the feasibility of jointly training a single Transformer on text and KGs.","reading_guide":"Focus on the definition of LIS and its algebraic foundation in Section 2 to understand the design of the structural address $A(s,e)$ and the journey operator $P_{j\\to i}$. The theoretical proofs in Section 3 regarding the flaws of additive encodings explain 'why it must be done this way.' This paper leans heavily on theory and architecture design, with weaker experimental validation, but its unified modeling philosophy is highly inspiring for pre-training research.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"提供了一种将图结构优雅地融入 Transformer 注意力机制的数学框架，对研究非序列数据（如知识图谱）的预训练有理论启发，但缺乏实证。","en":"Provides an elegant mathematical framework for integrating graph structures into Transformer attention, theoretically inspiring for pretraining on non-sequential data (e.g., KGs), though lacking empirical validation."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mahesh Godavarti"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Transformer Architecture","Graph Representation","Unified Modeling","Positional Encoding"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20278","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20278","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19363","title":"Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not","abstract":"The query and key projections $\\WQ,\\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivaria","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T19:35:47Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19363.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","math.NA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文讨论一个很具体的训练几何问题：Transformer attention 里的 query/key 投影矩阵通常用无约束的 AdamW 训练，但注意力分数只依赖 WQ WK^T，并且对 WQ、WK 的联合正交旋转不变。作者要验证的是，把 WQ、WK 约束在 Stiefel 流形上训练，是否比调好的欧式 AdamW 更稳、更泛化，尤其是在 attention 是性能瓶颈时是否有效。","importance":"对大规模预训练的直接证据有限，因为实验主要在 CIFAR-10 patch benchmark 和 modular arithmetic grokking 上。但论文有一个有用的提醒：优化器选择和参数几何不是独立问题，尤其是 Q/K 投影这种带有子空间结构和尺度退化风险的参数。对 LLM 预训练研究的启发在于，AdamW 的 weight decay、QK 投影尺度、attention collapse 之间可能存在被常规训练 recipe 掩盖的相互作用。它不是一个可以直接迁移到大模型训练的结论，但适合作为 attention 参数化和优化器设计的参考。","technical_approach":"方法只改 WQ、WK，其余参数仍用匹配的 AdamW。每个 attention head 的 WQ、WK 被约束到 St(d,r)，也就是列正交矩阵空间。更新时先把欧式梯度投影到 Stiefel 切空间，再用 Riemannian Adam 做步长计算。这里的 Adam 二阶矩不是逐元素保存，而是每个 frame 保存一个标量二阶矩 v_h，形式是对切向梯度 Frobenius 范数的指数滑动平均。这样做牺牲了逐坐标自适应性，但保留了 O(d) 等变性，避免坐标系选择影响更新方向。更新步还带 trust-region cap，限制切空间步长，然后用 polar retraction 拉回 Stiefel 流形，并对一阶动量做投影式 transport。作者还给出几个关键理论点：该更新在嵌入度量下对应最陡下降；对梯度尺度不敏感；条件性更好；满足 O(d) 等变。另一个机制结果是，weight decay 在 Stiefel 流形上的 Riemannian gradient 恒为零，因为 W 本身落在法空间里。因此，欧式 AdamW 中可能导致投影矩阵收缩和 attention geometry 破坏的 weight decay，在这个约束下不会沿流形产生实际更新。论文还澄清了 gauge 问题：attention 的不变性是 WQ、WK 的联合 O(r) 作用，不是每个矩阵独立的 Grassmann quotient；gauge 方向本身无梯度，因此去 gauge 不是使用 Riemannian 方法的主要理由。","experiments":"CIFAR-10 patch benchmark 中，作者控制架构、batch size、学习率 schedule 和非 Q/K 参数优化器，只比较 WQ、WK 的更新方式。Stiefel/Riemannian Adam 相对 Euclidean AdamW 有 +8.98 个百分点的 paired gain，12/12 个随机初始化均提升，t=60.6。数据规模从 1k 到 50k 变化时，收益没有出现 crossover，且 gap 随数据增加而扩大。消融显示，主要收益来自 scale-free、trust-bounded 的步长规则，而不是单纯的投影操作或等变性。modular arithmetic grokking 实验中，在 epoch 20000，Riemannian 方案保持 97.0% validation accuracy，而 baseline 为 61.1%。作者把这一结果和 weight decay 导致的 attention geometry collapse cycle 联系起来。论文还实现了 block-orthogonal multi-head extension，把多个 head 的 frame 折叠后一次 retraction，以强制 head 间子空间正交；初始化例子中 independent frames 的跨 head overlap max 为 0.387，folded retraction 后约为 9.7e-16。但该变体没有用于主要结果，也没有报告是否提升性能。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中对 joint O(r) invariance 和 Stiefel 约束动机的定义，再看 Method 里 Riemannian Adam 的三件事：切空间投影、标量二阶矩、trust-region cap 加 polar retraction。实验部分重点看 CIFAR-10 数据规模曲线、12 个 seed 的 paired comparison、以及 ablation，因为这些决定方法收益来自哪里。理论部分可重点看 weight decay 的 Riemannian gradient 为零这一命题，它比一般的流形优化推导更接近预训练训练动态。block-orthogonal multi-head extension 可以快速浏览，当前更像实现选项，不是经验证实的主结果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies whether the query and key projection matrices in attention should be optimized as unconstrained Euclidean parameters. Since attention scores depend on WQ WK^T and are invariant to a joint right-orthogonal rotation of WQ and WK, the authors test whether constraining these matrices to the Stiefel manifold changes optimization and generalization when attention is the limiting component.","importance":"The evidence is not from large-scale language model pretraining, so the result should not be treated as a direct LLM training recipe. Its value is more specific: it isolates an interaction between Q/K projection geometry, AdamW-style weight decay, and attention collapse. For pretraining researchers, the useful signal is that some projection matrices may not be well served by standard per-parameter Euclidean AdamW, especially when the relevant object is a subspace or a product such as WQ WK^T rather than the raw matrix entries.","technical_approach":"$2e","experiments":"On a CIFAR-10 patch benchmark, with all non-Q/K parameters controlled, the Stiefel optimizer gives a +8.98 percentage point paired gain over the Euclidean AdamW baseline, with 12 out of 12 starts improving and t=60.6. Across dataset sizes from 1k to 50k, the gap does not cross over and grows with more data. Ablations attribute the gain to the scale-free, trust-bounded step rule rather than to projection alone or to equivariance alone. On modular arithmetic grokking, the Riemannian arm reaches 97.0% validation accuracy at epoch 20000, compared with 61.1% for the baseline. The paper links this to weight-decay-driven collapse cycles in the baseline’s attention geometry. A block-orthogonal multi-head variant is implemented, forcing mutual orthogonality across heads by retracting a folded multi-head matrix. It reduces an initialization overlap example from 0.387 to about 9.7e-16, but it is not used for the reported accuracy results and is not evaluated as a performance intervention.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Start with the introduction’s discussion of joint O(r) invariance, because it clarifies why the relevant geometry is not a product of independent Grassmannians. Then read the optimizer construction: tangent projection, scalar second moment, trust-region clipping, polar retraction, and momentum transport. In the experiments, focus on the CIFAR data-scaling curve, the paired multi-seed comparison, and the ablation that assigns the gain to the trust-bounded scale-free update. The most useful theoretical piece for training-dynamics work is the proposition showing that weight decay has zero Riemannian gradient on Stiefel. The block-orthogonal head extension can be read lightly; it is an available variant, not an empirically validated main result.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是可直接上大模型训练的 recipe，但“Stiefel 上 weight decay 对 Q/K 实际失效”这个点值得记；先看理论命题和 grokking 图，别急着信泛化。","en":"Not a ready-to-use LLM recipe, but the claim that weight decay is effectively null on Stiefel-constrained Q/K is worth tracking; read the propositions and grokking result, but treat generalization claims cautiously."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Rubén Darío Guerrero"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["attention","stiefel-manifold","riemannian-optimization","grokking","transformer-geometry"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.19363","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19521","title":"LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata","abstract":"Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. reusable transition when run for more iterations than those directly supervised. The work separates two resources that are often conflated: extra computation from repeated application of a shared block, and memory carried across those repetitions. It asks whether the latest latent state is enough, whether an expanding attention cache helps, and whether bounded gated memory is a safer alternative.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining is indirect but clear for recurrent-depth models, continuous latent reasoning, and test-time compute. Parameter sharing makes additional iterations well-defined, but it does not make the learned transition dynamically stable. A larger memory interface can also hurt: CoTFormer’s retained KV history can interfere with a corrected current state rather than acting as a reliable external memory. This is a useful caution for designs that add persistent latent streams or recurrent reasoning loops to language models.","technical_approach":"The study compares three architectures. Block Universal Transformer carries only the current hidden sequence between recurrent applications. CoTFormer also keeps an expanding key-value cache from earlier recurrent steps. LSTM-UT adds a bounded gated memory, motivated by the need to preserve information without exposing all previous states unfiltered. Rule 30 cellular automata are used as a controlled depth-extrapolation task: the same shared block is repeated r times, matching r exact automaton updates, and the model is supervised at multiple depths. The main models use a GPT-2-style block with binary vocabulary, width 64, four attention heads, a 256-wide feed-forward layer, rotary positional encodings, and no dropout. By analyzing the score oracles of different diffusion processes, it proves that Uniform and Gaussian diffusion can sample in a number of steps proportional to the dual total correlation (DTC) of the data distribution using specific sampling algorithms. DTC measures the total entropy in the distribution not explained by local entropies and is typically much smaller than the sequence length d. DeepSeek-V4.1-Flas","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T09:43:10Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19969.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作解决的是超长上下文和长程 cache 管理解决，架构本身需要为 cache 生命周期服务。","technical_approach":"核心方案由三层组成。第一是 Causal Encoder-Decoder 架构。prefill 阶段主要通过 encoder 处理输入，decode 阶段 decoder 的 global KV 由 encoder 最终 hidden states 投影得到。这样做让模型在 prefill 每 token 激活约 8B 参数，在 decode 每 token 激活约 16B 参数。这个分配适合输入密集型负载，因为长程 agent 通常要反复读很长上下文，但每次生成不一定很长。第二是 Compressed Sparse Attention 2，即 CSA2。DeepSeek-V4 可理解为 SWA 局部处理骨架加压缩全局上下文；V4.1-Flash 沿着这个视角保留每层 SWA，但简化全局分支。CSA2 对 global KV、indexer K 和 Top-K indices 做跨层复用，层被静态分成 Full、Reindex、Reuse 三种模式。Full 层生成 global KV 并完成索引；Reindex 层复用前层 global KV，但用自己的 indexer Q 重新给共享 indexer K 打分并选择新的 Top-K；Reuse 层同时复用 global KV 和 Top-K indices，直接做稀疏注意力。每层仍保留自己的 global Q 和 SWA KV，因此不是简单共享整层注意力，而是只压掉全局 cache 中最重、重复度最高的部分。第三是 cache 精度和持久化策略。global KV 在训练中使用 FP4 cache，只带来很小的性能损失；部署时引入 SWA Bounded Replay，不再把 SWA KV 持久化到 SSD，而是只重放最近的 n_win 个 token 近似恢复 SWA KV。相比 DeepSeek-V4 中为了精确恢复需要重放 L × n_win 个 token，这里用少量额外 prefill 计算换取显著的持久化存储下降。除此之外，模型还把原来的 CSA-HCA 混合方案改成纯 CSA2，升级 Single-Pass mHC 和 Mega-mHC kernel，将 activation memory traffic 约减半，并集成 Engram 条件记忆模块和 DSpark 推测解码架构，分别增强能力和 decode 吞吐。","experiments":"关键实验围绕三件事展开：cache footprint、性能保持、部署代价。公开数字显示，CSA2 加 FP4 global KV 后，global KV runtime storage 约为 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的 1/4；再加上 SWA Bounded Replay 后，persistent KV footprint 约为 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的 1/8。模型规模为 552B backbone 参数，支持多模态输入和 1M token 上下文，prefill/decode 激活参数分别约为 8B/16B。消融结果的主要信息是：FP4 global KV cache 训练只造成边际性能下降；SWA Bounded Replay 只重放最近 n_win token，性能损失可以忽略；Single-Pass mHC 配合 Mega-mHC kernel 相比原四 kernel 实现减少约一半 activation memory traffic。能力侧的结论是，尽管 V4.1-Flash 比 V4-Flash 更激进地压缩 KV，并且结构更偏向部署效率，整体性能仍优于 V4-Flash。这一点比单项指标更重要，因为它说明 cache 压缩没有被当成纯损失性近似，而是与架构扩展、MoE 激活预算和条件记忆模块一起重新平衡。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中对 DeepSeek-V4 cache 组成的拆解，以及 Figure 1(b) 对 runtime/persistent KV footprint 的比较。随后重点看 CED 和 CSA2 章节：CED 决定 prefill/decode 计算预算如何分配，CSA2 决定 global KV 为什么可以跨层共享而不直接损害每层查询能力。Full/Reindex/Reuse 三种模式值得画成表格理解，尤其要区分“复用 KV”和“重新索引”的差别。FP4 KV cache 部分要关注它是在训练中使用，而不是事后量化。SWA Bounded Replay 部分值得细看，因为它给出了一种新的存储-计算交换方式：不持久化局部窗口 KV，用短回放近似恢复。mHC、Engram、DSpark 可以作为第二优先级阅读，它们更偏工程和整体系统吞吐，但能帮助理解 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 不是单一 KV 压缩技巧，而是一套面向长上下文服务的模型系统设计。","authors_note":"DeepSeek-AI；作者列表在给定内容中未完整显示。背景上延续 DeepSeek-V4、Compressed Sparse Attention、mHC、Engram 和 DSpark 等内部技术线，重点是长上下文 MoE、多模态基座和大规模推理部署。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"This work targets the KV-cache bottleneck in ultra-long-context and long-horizon agent serving. Sparse attention has already reduced part of the long-sequence compute cost, but at million-token context lengths the limiting factors shift to runtime KV in HBM, persistent KV in SSD or host memory, and the bandwidth needed to move and reload caches. DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash treats KV compression as a model-system co-design problem rather than an isolated inference trick.","importance":"The main value for foundation-model research is that serving constraints are pushed back into architecture and training. Instead of relying only on post-hoc cache quantization, pruning, or retrieval heuristics, the model changes how global context is represented and reused across layers, and trains with FP4 global KV caches. The reported scale is substantial: a 552B-backbone multimodal MoE model, native support for 1M-token contexts, about 8B activated parameters per token during prefill and 16B during decode, around 4× lower runtime KV storage and around 8× lower persistent KV storage than DeepSeek-V4-Flash at the same sequence length, while improving overall performance. The result is a useful signal that long-context cost curves are shaped by architecture, cache precision, and deployment policy together.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"The key reported results are centered on cache footprint, quality retention, and serving cost. CSA2 plus FP4 global KV reduces global runtime KV storage to roughly one quarter of DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Adding SWA Bounded Replay reduces persistent KV footprint to roughly one eighth. The model has 552B backbone parameters, supports multimodal inputs and 1M-token contexts, and activates about 8B parameters per token in prefill and 16B in decode. Ablations indicate that FP4 global KV training causes only marginal degradation, and bounded replay of only the most recent n_win tokens has negligible performance impact. The Single-Pass mHC and Mega-mHC implementation cuts activation memory traffic by about half compared with the previous four-kernel implementation. The capability result is also important: despite the more aggressive cache compression and the larger serving-oriented redesign, DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash improves overall performance over DeepSeek-V4-Flash.","reading_guide":"Read the introduction carefully for the decomposition of DeepSeek-V4 KV states and the motivation behind runtime versus persistent cache compression. Figure 1(b) is the main anchor for understanding the storage reductions. The most important technical sections are CED and CSA2. For CSA2, focus on the distinction between reusing global KV and recomputing indices through Reindex layers; that is the main reason the model can reduce cache duplication while preserving layer-specific querying. The FP4 KV section is worth close attention because the low-precision cache is used during training, not merely applied after training. The SWA Bounded Replay section is another key part: it gives a concrete storage-compute trade-off by replacing persisted local-window KV with short replay. The mHC, Engram, and DSpark sections are secondary but useful for understanding the complete serving-oriented model stack.","authors_note":"DeepSeek-AI; the full author list is not available in the provided content. The work continues the DeepSeek-V4 line, including compressed sparse attention, mHC, Engram, and DSpark, with a focus on long-context MoE, multimodal foundation models, and large-scale serving."},"recommendation":{"zh":"必须看系统表和消融；CED把prefill/decode激活量拆开，可能会改变长上下文MoE预训练的架构取舍。","en":"Read the system tables and ablations; separating prefill and decode activation in CED may change the architecture trade-off for long-context MoE pretraining."},"metadata":{"institutions":["DeepSeek-AI"],"authors":["DeepSeek-AI",":","Anyi Xu","B. Existing sandbox defense mechanisms often overlook the vulnerability of the inference engine itself as an attack surface, allowing models to trigger engine vulnerabilities via specially crafted tokens and achieve sandbox escape.","importance":"Exposes the severe security risks of the inference engine as a critical attack surface for LLM sandbox escapes. This is not just a theoretical vulnerability but an urgent security challenge in real-world deployments, serving as a crucial warning for the security architecture design of Inference-as-a-Service.","technical_approach":"The paper systematically explores model-driven environmental discovery, exploitation, and escape. First, it demonstrates the feasibility of a model identifying its local engine by cataloging fingerprintable behaviors of five popular inference engines. Second, it proposes a probing methodology based on output token selection and multi-round analysis to overcome limitations such as the model's inability to directly modify system prompts. Finally, it illustrates how a model can leverage engine fingerprint information to exploit vulnerabilities in the tokenization/detokenization layers, gain control of the inference engine process, and subsequently search for other vulnerabilities to achieve bare-metal control.","experiments":"Validates the practicality of inference engine exploitation through real-world incidents (e.g., sandbox escapes by OpenAI and Anthropic models) and fingerprint analysis of five popular inference engines. Neural drafts (e.g., EAGLE3) provide robust performance across diverse text settings, while copy-based methods achieve higher speedups in copy-intensive regimes by generating candidates faster and exploiting long repetition spans for near-perfect speculation. Surface n-gram overlap is often accidental rather than a real intent to copy, and false copy triggers can reduce throughput instead of improving it.","importance":"The work is mainly relevant to inference efficiency rather than pretraining. Its useful signal is that decoding contains heterogeneous regimes: copy-heavy spans and ordinary generative spans have different acceptance-length behavior. The target model’s hidden states can expose this regime information and can be used to control the decoding policy. This is relevant for long-context serving, code generation, document generation, and reasoning workloads.","technical_approach":"SwitchSD treats copying as a latent decoding mode. It trains a lightweight binary probe on the target model’s hidden representations to predict copy intent. The labeling rule is conservative: a position is labeled as copy mode if it belongs to a verbatim span of at least n_train=5 tokens that appeared earlier in the context; all other positions are labeled non-copy. Because such long repeated spans are sparse in ordinary data, the authors build a calibrated stimulus set with 1,000 prompts generated by Claude Sonnet, covering structures such as code boilerplate and repetitive linguistic templates. The target model generates completions, and the resulting hidden states are paired with copy labels. At inference time, SwitchSD uses context copying only when the probe predicts copy mode; otherwise it falls back to a neural drafter such as EAGLE3. The method therefore turns copy-based speculation from a brittle surface heuristic into a model-aware switch.","experiments":"The analysis shows that in reasoning-heavy tasks, more than 50% of copy attempts made by surface n-gram heuristics can be false positives. These accidental repetitions often produce zero-token acceptance and add verification overhead. With the hidden-state switch, SwitchSD improves throughput by up to about 15% over strong neural-drafting baselines across multiple benchmarks and model families. The experiments also show that true copy-favorable regions have much longer accepted continuations than normal generative regions, which explains why accurate switching matters. Ablations study probe design, training data, threshold selection, and drafting choices, with the main conclusion that internal representations provide a more reliable control signal than surface overlap.","reading_guide":"A 20-30 minute skim is enough. Full attention reads the entire growing history at every generation step, even when the next-token prediction does not benefit from distant context. The central question is whether a pretrained model’s current decoding state already reveals when a global read is useful, before performing that read.","importance":"This is mainly an inference-efficiency paper, but it has implications for foundation-model research. It suggests that long-context computation can be allocated at serving time without modifying pretrained weights. It also shows that hidden states learned during pretraining may contain meta-information about the value of additional context access. That matters for future long-context training recipes: architectural sparsity, hybrid attention, and serving-time routing can be complementary rather than mutually exclusive.","technical_approach":"On-Demand Attention uses a local-first decoding path. At each step, the model first runs a Local attention computation, using an initial-token-plus-recent-window pattern similar to StreamingLLM. A lightweight recall head then reads the available decoding state and predicts the relative benefit of recomputing the same step with Full attention. If the predicted benefit exceeds the routing criterion, ODA performs a Full recomputation for the current step; otherwise it accepts the Local result. The full historical KV cache is kept intact throughout decoding. ODA only changes which cached positions participate in the current attention computation, so future steps can still recall any earlier token. Only the recall head is trained; the pretrained backbone remains frozen. The paper also implements GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, which is important because conditional attention must reduce real memory traffic and kernel work, not just nominal FLOPs.","experiments":"The main results use Qwen3-1.7B, with additional experiments on Qwen3-8B, Gemma-4-12B-it, and the hybrid-attention Qwen3.5-2B. On RULER16K with Qwen3-1.7B, Local attention scores 19.23, Full attention scores 81.94, and ODA reaches 81.17 while invoking Full attention on only 41.6% of routed steps. In a controlled setup with 128K input tokens, 1K output tokens, and a 12.5% global-call rate, ODA reduces FLOPs for the main decoding operations by about 76% and achieves 1.98x single-request decoding throughput compared with native full attention. LongBench and larger-model results indicate that separately trained recall heads can produce similar quality-access trade-offs across model families. Results on Qwen3.5-2B show that step-level selective recall can complement hybrid-attention backbones.","reading_guide":"A focused skim should take 25 to 35 minutes. The cost section is important: check both FLOPs analysis and vLLM measurements, because dynamic methods often save theoretical compute without proportional wall-clock gains. The main technical lesson is that KV retention and KV access can be separated: the model can keep the full cache while reading it only when the current state predicts a useful gain.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"利用局部状态预测是否需要全局 Attention，思路巧妙且工程实现（vLLM 条件执行）扎实，做长文本推理优化的同学重点看其 recall head 的设计和训练目标。","en":"Cleverly uses local states to predict the need for global attention, backed by solid engineering (vLLM conditional execution); researchers working on long-context inference optimization should focus on the recall head design and training objective."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Peking University","Southern University of Science and Technology","Peng Cheng Laboratory","Affiliation:Peking University","Affiliation:Southern University of Science and Technology","Affiliation:Peng Cheng Laboratory"],"authors":["Haibo Feng","Ruiqi Liang","Hanyang Peng","Shiqi Yu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Long-Context Inference","Dynamic Attention","Inference Acceleration","vLLM"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20734","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20734","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19445","title":"From Models to Systems: A Comprehensive Survey of Efficient Multimodal Learning","abstract":"The rapid expansion of multimodal models has surfaced formidable bottlenecks in computation, memory, and deployment, catalyzing the rise of Efficient Multimodal Learning (EML) as a pivotal research frontier. de","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T04:31:34Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19683.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AR","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 内较小元素下溢，注意力图和跨模态融合被破坏。","importance":"对基座预训练本身不是核心贡献，但对 VLM 部署和训练后压缩有直接参考价值。它指出 VLM 量化不能简单复用 LLM 的 weight-only 或 activation quantization recipe，因为多模态 token 的分布差异会改变低比特格式的失效模式。对做 native multimodal model 的研究员，比较有用的点是：视觉 token 的长序列 KV cache 和激活压缩可能成为比权重量化更关键的瓶颈；量化格式需要显式适配 modality-induced outliers，而不是只在算法侧做平滑或旋转。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Micro-Inverted-Scaling, MiX。传统 MX 类格式是多个 mantissa 共享一个 exponent；MiX 反过来，让每个元素保留私有 exponent，同时在一个 block 内共享 mantissa。这样做的目的不是提高数值表达的平均精度，而是避免单个大离群值决定整个 block 的尺度。论文进一步采用 activation 用 MiX、weight 用 MX 的双格式设计。由于 MiX 的共享 mantissa 可以在 block dot product 中代数提出，硬件上可以把复杂乘法器替换为 bit-shifter 和 integer adder tree。这个设计把数值格式和 systolic accelerator 数据通路绑定起来，不只是一个离线量化算法。与 NVFP4、MXFP4+、AMXFP4、MicroScopiQ 的区别在于，MiX 不依赖多级 scale 或不规则借位机制，而是改变 block 内共享量的定义，换取更规则的硬件映射。","experiments":"论文在 Qwen2-VL、LLaVA-OneVision、MiniCPM-V 上做端到端评估，覆盖多个视觉语言 benchmark。文中给出的关键结果包括：4.5-bit MiX 可以匹配 NVFP4 baseline 的精度；在 TSMC 28nm CMOS 实现中，MiX 专用 accelerator 相比 NVFP4 的 PE area efficiency 提升 25%；相对 Focus，在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 和 MiniCPM-V-2.6 的六个 VLM benchmark 上平均加速 2.3×–4.5×，能耗降低 1.4×–2.9×。这些数字的重点在于 accuracy-PPA Pareto，而不是单纯 benchmark 分数。需要注意，结论依赖专用硬件格式和端到端量化路径，不能直接等价为通用 GPU 上的低比特推理收益。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–40 分钟。重点看 Background/Motivation 中对 VLM 激活分布的 profiling，以及 microscaling collapse 的例子；再看 MiX 数值格式定义和 MiX-MX dot product 如何化简到移位加法。硬件读者可以重点看 PE datapath、TSMC28 综合结果和与 NVFP4 / Focus 的 PPA 对比。做模型侧研究时，不必深追电路细节，重点抓住一个结论：多模态量化的主要困难来自视觉 token 离群值、文本 token 小幅特征和长 KV cache 的共同作用，端到端低比特需要格式、校准和硬件一起设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets end-to-end low-bit quantization for VLM inference on edge devices. The main failure mode is not ordinary quantization error, but a modality-driven dynamic-range mismatch. Visual tokens contain much larger outliers and broader activation ranges than text tokens. Standard microscaling or block floating-point formats assign one shared exponent to a block, so a single visual outlier can dominate the scale and push nearby smaller values into underflow, damaging attention patterns and multimodal fusion.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to VLM systems and compression. The useful lesson is that VLM quantization cannot be treated as a direct extension of LLM weight-only quantization. Dense visual tokens make activation and KV-cache compression a first-order deployment problem, and their activation statistics create different low-bit failure modes. For researchers working on native multimodal models, the paper provides a concrete example where modality-specific token distributions should influence numeric format and hardware design, not just post-hoc calibration.","technical_approach":"The proposed format is Micro-Inverted-Scaling, or MiX. Instead of grouping many mantissas under one shared exponent, as in conventional MX-style formats, MiX gives each element a private exponent and shares a mantissa within the block. This inversion is designed to tolerate large intra-block dynamic range without letting one outlier determine the entire block scale. The system uses a dual-format scheme: activations are represented in MiX, while weights use an MX-style format. Because the shared MiX mantissa can be factored out of block dot products, the accelerator maps the computation to bit shifts and integer adder trees rather than full multipliers. Compared with NVFP4, MXFP4+, AMXFP4, or MicroScopiQ, the main difference is that MiX changes what is shared inside the block, instead of adding nested scales, asymmetric scales, or irregular bit borrowing.","experiments":"The evaluation covers Qwen2-VL, LLaVA-OneVision, and MiniCPM-V on several vision-language benchmarks. The reported headline numbers are: a 4.5-bit MiX configuration matches the accuracy of an NVFP4 baseline; in a TSMC 28nm CMOS implementation, the MiX accelerator improves PE area efficiency by 25% over NVFP4; compared with Focus, MiX gives 2.3×–4.5× average speedup and 1.4×–2.9× energy reduction across six VLM benchmarks on LLaVA-OneVision-7B and MiniCPM-V-2.6. The important axis is the accuracy-PPA tradeoff. The results should be read as evidence for a hardware-format co-design, not as a drop-in low-bit recipe for commodity GPUs.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 30–40 minutes. Focus on the motivation section that profiles VLM activation distributions and explains microscaling collapse. Then read the MiX format definition and the MiX-MX dot-product derivation, since that is where the algorithmic idea connects to the multiplier-free datapath. For model researchers, the most useful takeaway is the failure mode: visual outliers, small text features, and long visual KV caches interact in a way that makes end-to-end VLM quantization different from LLM quantization.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"针对 VLM 异常值的创新量化格式，反转了共享指数的常规做法，做量化和端侧部署的研究员值得看其格式设计和硬件映射。","en":"Innovative quantization format targeting VLM outliers by inverting the shared exponent convention; researchers in quantization and edge deployment should check its format design and hardware mapping."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Cornell University","Cornell Tech","Cornell Tech, Cornell University"],"authors":["Yuan Liao","Jae-sun Seo"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Quantization","Vision-Language Models","Edge Computing","Microscaling","Hardware Acceleration"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19683","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.19683","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.19207","title":"MeshKV: A Network-on-Chip KV Cache Fabric for Scalable Transformer Decoding Accelerators","abstract":"Autoregressive transformer decoding is constrained by irregular key-value (KV) cache movement on tiled accelerators. Prior compression and DRAM-placement systems still concentrate traffic on centralized memory paths that bottleneck long-context serving. We present MeshKV, a KV cache fabric that moves blocks as packetized flows over a lightweight NoC. It co-designs (i) TaKV affine striping to spread homes and cut hotspot load, (ii) Mare multicast with verified duplicate suppression, and (iii) Pad","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-16T10:34:39Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19207.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是长上下文自回归解码时，KV cache 在 tiled accelerator 片上网络中的搬运瓶颈。问题不只是“显存带宽不够”，而是集中式 KV 访问路径会把流量压到少数路由和 bisection link 上，导致计算 tile 等数据。","importance":"它更偏高效推理硬件，而不是预训练方法论文。对基座研究的启发在于：长上下文模型的可用性受 KV cache 数据布局和片上互连强约束，不能只从 attention 算法或 KV 压缩看成本。对于做长上下文预训练和 serving-aware 模型设计的人，这篇论文提供了一个较具体的硬件视角：如果解码阶段 batch 很小，prefill 中可复用的 SRAM locality 会消失，KV 访问模式会变成跨 mesh 的 all-to-few gather。","technical_approach":"MeshKV 把 KV cache 当作片上通信对象来设计，而不是只考虑放在 DRAM/HBM 还是压缩。方案有三部分。第一是 TaKV，把 layer 和 token segment 组成 macro-block 后按 affine mapping π(ℓ,s) 分散到 mesh 上；stride 取与维度互素的形式，例如 a=W+1，使连续层和连续 segment 不落在同一热点区域。它明确指出平均 hop 数不会因为换 home tile 而下降，收益来自降低最大链路负载和注入热点。每 128 个 decode step 还基于 credit counter 做轻量 occupancy swap，目标是降低链路队列负载平方和。第二是 Mare，把相同 segment 的请求在短窗口内合并，再从 home tile 建 multicast tree 分发 KV_DATA；同时用 duplicate suppression 避免重复 payload，用 VN0/VN1 分离 PART 和 KV_FETCH/KV_DATA，降低死锁风险。第三是 Pad，将 prefetch、tile multiply、streaming softmax 做流水化，用 credit-aligned FIFO 隐藏 NoC round-trip latency。它和 KV compression、paged KV KV-cache movement during long-context autoregressive decoding on tiled accelerators. The main bottleneck is not only off-chip memory bandwidth; centralized KV access creates hot injection points and bisection pressure inside the on-chip mesh.","importance":"This is primarily an inference-hardware paper, not a pretraining-method paper. Its relevance to foundation-model research is the serving constraint it makes explicit: long-context decoding can be limited by KV layout and on-chip communication, even when the attention computation itself is optimized. For researchers working on long-context models, it is a useful reminder that prefill locality does not carry over to batch-1 decoding.","technical_approach":"MeshKV treats the KV cache as a communication fabric. It has three main components. TaKV places layer-token macro-blocks with an affine mapping π(ℓ,s), using a coprime stride such as a=W+1 to spread homes across the mesh. The paper is careful about the mechanism: the expected hop count to a fixed destination does not improve; the gain comes from reducing peak injection and bisection load. It also applies lightweight occupancy swaps every 128 decode steps based on existing credit counters. Mare coalesces requests for the same segment within a short window, then multicasts KV_DATA from the home tile using a spanning tree, with duplicate suppression and a VN0/VN1 split to separate partial-result traffic from fetch/data traffic. Pad overlaps prefetch, tile multiplication, and streaming softmax using credit-aligned FIFOs. Compared with KV compression or DRAM paging, the optimization target is the movement pattern of cache-resident or locally promoted KV blocks over the NoC.","experiments":"The evaluation uses an 8×8 FPGA implementation with LLaMA-2-7B and Mistral-7B at 8K–32K context lengths. The paper gives concrete sizing: for LLaMA-2-7B with 32 layers, 32 heads, head dimension 128, and FP16 KV, the 32K KV slab is about 16 GiB. With P=64, a macro-block covering all heads and both K/V is about 1 MB. On the U280 setup, HBM2 is 8 GB at roughly 460 GB/s and DDR4 is 32 GB, so the full 32K KV slab cannot live in HBM alone; tile-local URAM acts as a four-bank streaming window. A key motivation result is that at 32K, 78% of segment requests arrive at the same router within 12 cycles, which makes a small coalescing window practical. The paper also reports that centralized designs spend 40–60% of link cycles under back-pressure. Its useful signal for foundation-model research is the treatment of representation mismatch during model transfer. Local layer fitting is not enough: if the training-time feature distribution differs from the inference-time feature distribution, deeper layers can amplify the error. The same issue can appear in layer dropping, student initialization, and continual-pretraining compression pipelines.","technical_approach":"The method builds on layer-wise knowledge transfer. The teacher and student are partitioned into layer segments, and each segment is optimized to transfer intermediate representations. The paper formalizes cumulative error with a Lipschitz-style bound: the local error measured when a student segment is fed teacher features differs from the actual error when student segments are composed sequentially. This motivates a layer-wise curriculum. Training starts with an easier or broader optimization target and gradually reallocates more budget to deeper, more error-prone layers. The design is meant to use a fixed optimization budget where it matters most, instead of treating all segments uniformly. The system also uses feature caching with multi-threading to avoid redundant forward passes, and a direction-based feature loss to emphasize angular alignment rather than raw magnitude matching.","experiments":"The experiments cover BERT, GPT-2, LLaMA-family models, and Qwen, spanning encoder and decoder-only settings. Each observation follows from its own earlier actions, so the states it meets late in an episode are partly of its own making. Endpoint success mixes two changes: where the a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T03:27:53Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19636.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在闭环环境（如多步Agent任务）中，RL 训练带来的性能提升究竟是因为模型学会了到达更有利的状态（Reach），还是因为在相同状态下做出了更好的决策（Solve）？现有的端到端评测无法区分这两者，因为早期动作会改变后续状态，导致 SFT 和 RL 模型在不同状态下被评估（内生状态问题）。","importance":"这篇工作直击 Agent RL 评估的核心痛点。它指出，仅看最终成功率会掩盖 RL 的真实作用机制，甚至在过滤到共同到达的状态时会引入选择偏差。通过解耦状态访问和状态条件价值，该研究为理解 RL 在多步推理/交互任务中的具体贡献提供了清晰的分析框架，对后续 Agent RL 的训练目标设计和评测方法有重要指导意义。","technical_approach":"提出 Checkpoint Handoff 评估协议。核心思路是将任务拆分为“到达者”（Reacher）和“解决者”（Solver）。在环境验证的中间状态（Frontier），克隆当前环境、历史和剩余预算，然后让 SFT 和 RL 模型从完全相同的状态继续执行。Reach 衡量到达特定中间状态的频率，Solve 衡量在到达该状态条件下的完成率。通过交叉组合 {SFT, RL} 作为 Reacher 和 Solver，在不重新训练的情况下分离策略诱导的状态分布和状态条件价值。","experiments":"在 TravelPlanner 和 ALFWorld 两个基准上，使用两个独立的 SFT/RL 训练管线进行实验。结果表明：1) Solver 从 SFT 切换到 RL 带来的增益，高度依赖于 Reacher 是 SFT 还是 RL（存在正向交互作用）；2) 在 ALFWorld 的精确重放实验中，RL 的优势同时来源于更高的 Reach（到达有用状态）和更好的 Solve（在相同状态下决策更优）；3) 独立测量的 Reach 和 Solve 差距可以预测 Solver 增益对 Reacher 的依赖性。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Section 2 对内生状态问题（Endogenous State Problem）的清晰定义，以及 Section 3 中 Checkpoint Handoff 的具体实现机制。图 1 直观展示了问题的核心。实验部分关注 ALFWorld 的精确重放结果（Section 5），这部分最能体现 Reach 和 Solve 的解耦效果。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In closed-loop environments (e.g., multi-step Agent tasks), does the performance gain from RL training stem from reaching more advantageous states (Reach) or making better decisions from identical states (Solve)? Existing end-to-end evaluations conflate these two factors because early actions alter subsequent states, causing SFT and RL models to be evaluated from different states (the endogenous state problem).","importance":"This work addresses a critical blind spot in Agent RL evaluation. It demonstrates that relying solely on endpoint success rates obscures the true mechanisms of RL and that filtering for commonly reached states introduces selection bias. By decoupling state visitation from state-conditional value, this research provides a rigorous analytical framework for understanding RL's specific contributions in multi-step reasoning/interaction tasks, offering significant guidance for future training objective design and evaluation methodologies.","technical_approach":"Introduces the Checkpoint Handoff evaluation protocol. The core idea is to separate the task into a 'Reacher' and a 'Solver'. At an environment-verified intermediate state (Frontier), the environment, history, and remaining budget are cloned, allowing both SFT and RL models to continue execution from identical inputs. 'Reach' measures the frequency of arriving at the intermediate state, while 'Solve' measures the completion rate conditional on arrival. By crossing {SFT, RL} as Reachers and Solvers, the protocol isolates policy-induced state distribution from state-conditional value without retraining.","experiments":"Experiments were conducted on TravelPlanner and ALFWorld using two independent SFT/RL training pipelines. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T07:12:42Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_19799.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文指出，LLM 分配随策略、任务和总预算变化；单 seed 的方法排名会和多 seed 预算下的排名不一致；某些任务上单 seed 看起来最差的策略，在 40 seeds 下反而最好；常用的 200 次深度并不总是合理，很多情况下更浅迭代加更多 seed 能取得更高期望 best score。论文还指出 transaction scheduling evaluator 存在不完整性，会放过 is not mainly about foundation-model pretraining, but it is relevant to LLM-based automated discovery pipelines. Any workflow that uses LLMs to search for programs, heuristics, data-generation policies, reward functions, or system kernels has the same best-of-budget structure. The useful lesson is statistical: the object being evaluated is not one trajectory, but the expected best result under a budget allocation. Reporting only one seed can select the wrong method.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose the budget into width k and depth t, with B=k×t. Each seed produces a score trajectory; the per-seed value at depth t is the running maximum up to t, and the multi-seed value is the maximum over k sampled seeds. They log 40 seeds with 200 iterations each and then compute the finite-sample expected best score for every k,t split using exact order statistics. This avoids Monte Carlo resampling noise and produces a full budget surface for each strategy and task. The main methodological difference from common practice is that width and depth are treated as tunable evaluation variables, not as fixed defaults.","experiments":"The experiments use the ADRS engine with gpt-5-mini while holding the model, objective, and evaluator fixed. The benchmark contains five optimization tasks, including PRISM, Cloudcast, and transaction scheduling from ADRS-Bench. The key empirical result is that the best width-depth split changes with the task, the strategy, and the total budget. Method rankings can invert: a strategy that is weak at one seed can become strongest at forty seeds.
